Maximum count can be reduced by "balancing active lags". We can identify lags with C > current_best - 1? Since integer. For each active lag L, to reduce C_L, we need flip endpoints of contributing pairs. We can propose targeted flips:
For positive lag d (C[off+d] high): high C means many pairs (i,i+d) with h_i=0,h_{i+d}=1 (increasing). To reduce, flip either some 1 at higher positions (to 0) and some 0 elsewhere (to 1). For positive lag, C counts 0 at lower and 1 at higher. If flip lower zero to 1, then pair becomes 1,1, not counted.    - Flip p1 (1->0), p0 (0->1), update C twice, newmax. If newmax < best, keep and reset no_improve; else revert.

We can implement a robust local search function binary_local_search(h, max_trials, rng):
- n, off.
- C = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').astype(float)
- best = int(np.round(C.max()))
- best_h = h.copy()
- no_improve =0
- For trial in range(max_trials): time check.
   - mode: 80% active, 20% random.
   - If active:
       active_lags = indices where C >= best - active_tol (e.g., 2). If none all? Use best. Choose idx among active with some weight maybe linear.
       L=abs(idx-off); d_sign.
       If L==0 (C0=0 for binary) ignore.
       sample up to 10 random i in valid range until contributing pair.
       Determine p_one, p_zero.
       flip_pair(p_one, p_zero, C, h, directions?) p_one must be 1, p_zero 0.
       newmax = int(np.max(C)) maybe use np.max C. If newmax < best-? accept. If newmax == best, maybe accept if random active? To avoid many O(n) max? np.max C O(n) okay. We can maintain bestmax using heuristics? We'll just np.max.
       If not accepting, revert flips (apply with -δ and restore h).
   - Random: choose random one p1, random zero p0, flip, newmax. Accept if <= best and maybe energy lower.
   - If best < previous, store. If best target reached (<=target_count) can break.
- Return best_h, best_count.

Need efficient revert. We can write flip_update(h, C, p, delta) that updates C and h. To revert a pair flip, apply flip_update to p_one with delta +1 (restore one) and p_zero with delta -1 (restore zero) in reverse? Since updates are linear for current h. If we applied p_one (1->0 δ=-1) then p_zero (0->1 δ=+1), to revert should apply p_zero (+1? back 1->0 δ=-1?) Wait after pair, p_one=0, p_zero=1. To revert, flip p_one from0 to1 δ=+1 and p_zero from1 to0 δ=-1. The update uses current h, so order can be any. We can store a small history of (p, old_bit) and revert reverse using flip_update(p, +1 if old 1 else -1). Or simpler make h copy before trial? Too expensive for many. But pair flips small; we can write flip_bit and track. We'll update h inside; to revert, call flip_bit(p_one, +1) (since it is 0->1) and flip_bit(p_zero, -1). Need ensure after first flip, C updated; restoring should bring C exactly. The update formula is valid for any h (even unbalanced) if flip δ changes bit by ±1 and current h. So fine.

Flip function top-level:
def flip_binary(h, C, p, delta):
    n=len(h); off=n-1
    # delta +1 means 0->1, -1 means 1->0.
    # Use float h? h could be int/float. We'll store h as float64 or int8. Use float for (1-h) operations. C float.
    # Must not modify old h before slices? We use h; after slices set h[p]+=delta.
    C[off:off+p+1] += delta * (1.0 - h[:p+1])[::-1]
    C[off:off+n-p] += -delta * h[p:n]
    start = off - (n-1-p)
    C[start:off+1] += delta * (1.0 - h[p:n])[::-1]
    C[off-p:off+1] += -delta * h[:p+1]
    h[p] += delta
    return C

For fixed other h, each f_j is linear in h_x (because product only between pairs; h_x appears linearly? Let's check f_j = h_x(1-h_{x+d}) + h_{x-d}(1-h_x) + terms. This is linear in h_x! Yes for a single coordinate, with all other variables fixed, each f_j is affine in h_x (coefficient either (1-h_y)+? two terms). The maximum over j of affine functions; minimizing over h_x separately is convex piecewise linear (max of lines). Thus coordinate-wise minimization is exact and can be done by checking breakpoints. This is powerful! We can perform coordinate descent on h (continuous) exactly: for each coordinate x, given others fixed, objective F(h_x)=max_j (a_j h_x + b_j), where a_j,b_j depend on neighbors and weights, subject 0≤h_x≤1 and sum constraint? Sum constraint couples all; but we can maintain sum by adjusting another coordinate or include sum equality. Coordinate descent on constrained box with sum equality: For a coordinate h_p, changing it breaks sum. We can change p and q pair simultaneously (like sum-preserving) or use a penalty for sum. Pair coordinate optimization: choose pair (p,q) with p increased, q decreased to keep sum? The objective as function of Δ (h_p+=Δ,h_q-=Δ) is max of affine functions in Δ (each f_j linear in Δ), on interval such that bounds hold. We can minimize exactly by evaluating at breakpoints (slopes changes) O(n #lags). This could be more robust than gradient, and can reduce C5. It also allows continuous values. Pair swaps (mass transfer) maintain sum. This is analogous binary but continuous. We can use top active lags perhaps.

But if direction based on active gradient at current, moving mass from q (high derivative) to p (low derivative) should decrease.

Algorithm iteration modes:
1. "active pair descent": choose a lag from max set; choose a random contributing pair (p,q) that is oriented as needed; line search t. Accept if F decreases.
2. "gradient pair descent": compute active gradient G; choose p argmin, q argmax; line search.
3. "random pair descent": choose random p zero, q one (or any p,q) line search.
This is like local search with exact 1D block moves.

def add_point_deriv(s, h, x, sign):
   n=len(h); off=n-1
   s[off] += sign*(1 - 2*h[x])
   if x > 0: s[off+1:off+x+1] += sign*(1 - h[:x])[::-1]
   r = n-1-x
   if r > 0:
       s[off+1:off+r+1] += sign*(-h[x+1:n])
       s[x:off] += sign*(1 - h[x+1:n])[::-1]
       s[off-x:off] += sign*(-h[:x])
But first positive L=1..x: use h[:x] reversed (x elements). second positive L=1..r: stop off+1 + r = off+ n - x? length r, add -h[x+1:n]. third negative L=1..r: slice x:off (length off-x = n-1-x = r) add (1-h[x+1:n])[::-1]. fourth negative L=1..x: slice off-x:off length x add -h[:x]. This matches earlier. For x=0, only r=n-1; s[1:off+1] += -h[1:n], s[0:off] += (1-h[1:n])[::-1], s[off:off] no. C0 derivative at off: 1-2h0.

   Return best.

Function add_point_derivative(s,h,x,sign): top-level, no closure.
Function accumulate_gradient(h, C, beta, max_lags):
   n=len(h); off=n-1; m=2*n-1
   F = C.max()
   # weights maybe exp(beta*(C-F)) / sum where C-F<=0. If beta=0 uniform? But we want top.
   w = np.exp(np.clip(beta*(C-F), -50, 0.0))
   wsum = w.sum(); if wsum==0: weight only argmax.
   w = w / wsum
   g = zeros(n)
   # process lags with w > 1e-6 maybe top.
   idx = np.where(w > 1e-6)[0]
   # to reduce loops, sort by w desc and process until sum processed >=0.999
   order = idx[np.argsort(w[idx])[::-1]]? Actually idx sorted.
   remaining_weight=1.0
   for k in order:
      wk=w[k]; L=abs(k-off)
      if wk < 1e-8: break maybe after process top enough.
      if L==0: g += wk*(1-2*h)
      else if k>off: # positive
         r=n-L
         g[L:] += wk*(1-h[:r])
         g[:r] -= wk*h[L:]
      else:
         r=n-L
         g[:r] += wk*(1-h[L:])
         g[L:] -= wk*h[:r]
      remaining_weight-=wk
      if remaining_weight < 0.01: break
   return g
This gradient smooths over lags. For beta large, g from active. But if beta=0, uniform all lags, may be expensive if process all? We can set min_weight 1e-5 and max_lags to process. Since m≤600, processing 600 loops*n (600*256=153k) per gradient, maybe okay for few. But line search per gradient. We can set beta=10 and process weights>1e-4; maybe 50 lags.

Line search pair function details:
def pair_line_search(h, C, p, q, t_min=-inf, t_max=inf, grid=97):
   n=len(h); off=n-1; m=2*n-1
   if p==q: return 0, C, h
   lo = t_min; hi=t_max
   # bound by [ -min(h[p],1-h[q]), min(1-h[p],h[q]) ]
   boundslo = -min(h[p], 1.0 - h[q])
   boundshi = min(1.0 - h[p], h[q])
   lo = max(lo, boundslo); hi=min(hi,boundshi)
   if hi - lo <= 1e-12:
       return 0.0, C, h
   s = zeros(m)
   add_point_derivative(s,h,p,+1)
   add_point_derivative(s,h,q,-1)
   c = zeros(m)
   L=abs(p-q)
   if L>0:
       c[off+L]=1.0; c[off-L]=1.0
   # t grid include 0
   # Use coarse grid, maybe symmetrical around 0. But if interval small, fewer points.
   # Ensure 0 included: use np.linspace(lo,hi,grid); index closest 0 maybe. We can evaluate at t values; current F = max C.
   tg = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid)
   # To speed, if c has only two ones, c*broadcast can be avoided? But fine.
   # However creating 2D array each call is overhead. Could evaluate F for t in scalar loop if grid 33? Vectorized likely faster.
   # For m ~ 399, grid 95 -> 38k; okay.
   val = C[None, :] + tg[:, None] * s[None, :] + (tg*tg)[:, None] * c[None, :]
   ft = val.max(axis=1)
   # due floating, current at t=0 maybe C.max. Ensure t=0 value not less than F? If grid doesn't include exactly 0, best might positive even if actual at 0 best. Include t=0 explicitly: append 0 and recompute ft for 0 = currentF; find best index.
   currentF = C.max()
   best_t = 0.0; best_val = currentF
   # find min ft
   i=int(np.argmin(ft)); if ft[i] < best_val - 1e-12: best_t=tg[i]
   # refine around best_t if not 0:
   if best_t !=0:
       step=abs(hi-lo)/grid
       lo2=max(lo,best_t-step); hi2=min(hi,best_t+step)
       # But need include 0? no, refine.
       tg2=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,33)
       val2=...
       ft2=...
       i2=argmin; if ft2[i2] < best_val: best_t=tg2[i2]; best_val=ft2[i2]
   # apply with clipping
   if abs(best_t)>1e-14:
       t=best_t
       # ensure within bound with eps
       if h[p]+t < -1e-12 or h[q]-t < -1e-12 or h[p]+t > 1+1e-12 or h[q]-t >1+1e-12: t=0
       if abs(t)>1e-14:
           C2 = C + best_t*s + (best_t*best_t)*c
           h2 = h.copy()? To avoid copying every time? We can update in place if we plan to accept. But line search may reject; we can update C in place and h in place, then if best_val >= currentF revert? We can just assume best_val < currentF, apply. If no improvement, no apply. So function can return t and array s/c? But line_search called and if improves, we need update. To avoid passing arrays, let function update in place only if improving, else leave. But if it evaluates ft and best, we can apply by modifying h,C. However h used in s computation; after update okay. We can return new F and t.
           C += best_t*s + (best_t*best_t)*c
           h[p] += best_t; h[q] -= best_t
           # clip tiny
           if h[p] <0: h[p]=0; elif >1: h[p]=1; same q. If clip, sum may drift; but tiny.
           C = np.maximum(C,0)
           return best_t, best_val
   return 0.0,currentF

Continuous optimizer function:
def continuous_pair_opt(h, max_steps, rng, deadline, target_c5=...):
   n=len(h); h=h.copy(); C=compute_corr(h); F=C.max(); best=h.copy(); bestF=F; bestC=C.copy()
   no_improve=0
   step=0
   while step<max_steps and time.time()<deadline:
       F = C.max()
       # Active set
       mode = step % 10; choose
       if mode <5: active pair
       elif mode<8: gradient
       else random
       got=False
       if active:
           tol = max(0.25, 0.02*F) # corr units
           act = np.where(C >= F - tol)[0]
           if len(act)==0: act=[argmax]
           # choose weighted by C maybe: idx = act[rng.randint(len(act))]
           p,q,orient = random_contrib_cont(h, idx, off, rng)
           if p is not None:
               curF=F
               t,newF=pair_line_search(h,C,p,q,t_min=0.0,t_max=1.0,grid=81)
               if t!=0: F=newF; got=True
       if not got and mode maybe gradient:
           g=accumulate_gradient(h,C,beta=8.0)
           p=int(np.argmin(g)); q=int(np.argmax(g))
           if p!=q:
              curF=F
              t,newF=pair_line_search(h,C,p,q, grid=97)
              if t!=0: F=newF; got=True
       if not got:
           # random pair: choose p low, q high maybe
           p = int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n)); while p==q: q=...
           # To bias, sort? no.
           t,newF=pair_line_search(h,C,p,q,grid=65)
           if t!=0: F=newF; got=True
       if got:
           # Recompute F exactly? pair_line gave best_val. Due C update, C.max should match.
           F = C.max()
           if F < bestF - 1e-12:
               bestF=F; best=h.copy(); bestC=C.copy(); no_improve=0
           else:
               no_improve +=1
               # If C updated but not global best? Actually if got t reduced current F but current F may still >bestF? Since best is global above current? We maintain current from best? We might allow current to degrade? No, pair_line_search only improves current F (strict). If current F > bestF, maybe because current state is not best? We should keep working from current state even if temporarily worse? But we only accept improvements from current, so current F monotonically decreases. bestF tracked. If no global improvement beyond best? If current best, got means improve best. If current not best? We reset current to best after no-improve? We'll manage.
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 500:
              # restart from best with perturbations
              h = best.copy(); C=bestC.copy()
              # random small perturbation
              for _ in range(rng.randint(5,20)):
                 p,q random; t=rng.uniform(-0.1,0.1) within bounds; h[p]+=t;h[q]-=t; project? But project may break sum? We can perform random pair moves with line search? Simpler use pair_line_search random to improve from perturbed? Maybe no.
              C=compute_corr(h); F=C.max()
              no_improve=0
   return best,bestF

For active pair random_contrib_cont:
def sample_active_pair(h, idx, off, rng):
   n=len(h); Lq=abs(idx-off)
   if Lq==0: return None,None
   r=n-Lq
   if idx>off: # positive lag: term h[i+L]*(1-h[i]), p=i (increase lower zero), q=i+L (decrease higher one)
       for _ in range(8):
          i=rng.randint(r)
          val=h[i+Lq]*(1-h[i])
          if val > 0.05: return i,i+Lq
       # brute argmax
       vals = h[Lq:n]*(1-h[:r])
       i=int(np.argmax(vals))
       if vals[i] <= 1e-12: return None,None
       return i,i+Lq
   else: # negative: term h[i]*(1-h[i+L]), p=i+L (increase higher zero), q=i (decrease lower one)
       for _ in range(8):
          i=rng.randint(r)
          val=h[i]*(1-h[i+Lq])
          if val > 0.05: return i+Lq,i
       vals = h[:r]*(1-h[Lq:n])
       i=int(np.argmax(vals))
       if vals[i]<=1e-12: return None,None
       return i+Lq,i

def threshold_to_binary(h, n):
   # choose top n/2 indices as 1
   vals = h
   k = n//2
   # if h already binary exact ones k, return
   if np.allclose(vals, np.round(vals)) and np.sum(round)==k: return round
   order = np.argpartition(-vals, k-1)[:k] if k>0 else []
   out = zeros(n); out[order]=1; return out

Let's design block binary generator top-level:
def make_block_binary(n, rng):
   # choose number of runs R between 8 and 40 maybe even.
   R = int(rng.integers(8, 41))
   start = rng.randint(2)
   # lengths: generate for R positive integers via random cuts of n.
   cuts = np.sort(rng.choice(np.arange(1,n), size=R-1, replace=False))
   lengths = np.diff(np.concatenate(([0], cuts, [n])))
   # assign bits alternating starting start
   # To balance, if total ones not n/2, adjust by flipping bits in longest runs.
   bits = np.where(np.arange(R)%2 == start, 1.0, 0.0)
   h = np.repeat(bits, lengths)
   # adjust count exactly n/2 by flipping bits in smallest? We'll convert to binary.
   target=n//2; cur=int(h.sum())
   if cur>target: flip ones to zeros choosing those in shortest? Random indices until.
   elif cur<target: flip zeros to ones.
   return h

def project_feasible(h, n):
   h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
   h = np.clip(h,0,1)
   target = n/2.0
   diff = target - h.sum()
   # iterative water filling to shift without violating bounds? Simple uniform may overshoot. Use function adjust_sum:
   for _ in range(5):
      if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
      if diff>0:
         mask = h < 1 - 1e-15
         cnt=mask.sum()
         if cnt==0: break
         add = diff/cnt
         # if add would exceed 1 for some, could loop; simple
         h[mask] += add
         h = np.clip(h,0,1)
      else:
         mask = h > 1e-15
         cnt=mask.sum()
         if cnt==0: break
         add = diff/cnt
         h[mask] += add
         h=np.clip(h,0,1)
      diff = target - h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-14:
      # adjust smallest room
      if diff>0: mask = h <1; else h>0
      if mask.any():
         h[mask] += diff/mask.sum()
         h=np.clip(h,0,1)
      # final tiny to argmin distance
      diff=target - h.sum()
      if abs(diff)>1e-15:
         if diff>0: idx=np.argmin(1-h) else idx=np.argmin(h)
         h[idx] += diff
         h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   return h
But uniform add may not fix exactly due clipping; loop fine. For target n/2, total room enough.

def threshold_to_binary(h, n):
    target = n//2
    h = np.asarray(h,float)
    # If close integer and sum target
    closed = np.rint(h)
    if np.all(np.abs(h-closed) < 1e-9) and int(np.sum(closed)) == target:
        return closed.astype(float)
    if target <=0: return zeros
    if target >= n: return ones
    # Use argpartition for all negative? If target==n, no.
    part = np.argpartition(-h, target-1)[:target]
    out=np.zeros(n)
    out[part]=1.0
    return out

def make_random_binary(n,rng):
   h=np.zeros(n)
   target=n//2
   if target>0:
      idx=rng.choice(n, size=target, replace=False)
      h[idx]=1.0
   return h

make_smooth_random:
def make_smooth_random(n, rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
   h=0.5*np.ones(n)
   for _ in range(3):
       h += rng.uniform(0.1,0.3)*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.uniform(0.5,4.0)*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   h = np.clip(h,0,1)
   return project_feasible(h,n)

def make_block_binary(n, rng):
   R = int(rng.randint(8, min(40, n//2)+1))
   start = int(rng.randint(2))
   cuts = np.sort(rng.choice(np.arange(1,n), size=R-1, replace=False))
   lengths = np.diff(np.concatenate(([0], cuts, [n])))
   bits = np.where((np.arange(R) % 2) == start, 1.0, 0.0)
   h = np.repeat(bits, lengths)
   target=n//2
   diff=target - int(np.sum(h))
   # adjust by flipping bits in runs? Simple random flips respecting count.
   if diff>0:
      zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
      if len(zeros)>=diff:
          flip=rng.choice(zeros, size=diff, replace=False); h[flip]=1.0
   elif diff<0:
      ones=np.flatnonzero(h==1)
      flip=rng.choice(ones, size=-diff, replace=False); h[flip]=0.0
   return h

flip_binary: as derived. Need ensure C length m=2n-1. Function in-place. Use float h, C.
def flip_binary(h,C,p,delta):
   n=len(h); off=n-1
   d=float(delta)
   if d ==0: return
   if p > 0:
       C[off+1:off+p+1] += d * (1.0 - h[:p])[::-1]
   r = n-1-p
   if r > 0:
       C[off+1:off+r+1] += -d * h[p+1:n]
       C[p:off] += d * (1.0 - h[p+1:n])[::-1]
       C[off-p:off] += -d * h[:p]
   h[p] += d
   # if binary, maybe clip exactly? Delta ±1, h may be 0/1. Clip tiny:
   if h[p] < -1e-12: h[p]=0.0
   elif h[p] > 1.0+1e-12: h[p]=1.0
   # C off0 unchanged; could clip negative small later.
   return
Check slice C[off+1:off+r+1] length r, if r=0 skip. For r>0, off+r+1 = off+n-p. Good. C[p:off] uses p maybe 0: C[0:off] length off=n-1. r=n-1, h[p+1:n] length n-1, good. C[off-p:off] for p=0 gives C[off:off] empty; we also have p>0 guard. Good.
For negative one slice: C[p:off] += d*(1-h[p+1:n])[::-1]. For p=n-1, p=off, slice off:off empty, skip due r=0. Good.

Continuous pair opt details:
Function cpp? We'll ensure no nested.
def continuous_pair_opt(h, max_steps, deadline, rng, target_c5=None):
   h=project_feasible(h.copy?,n)
   C=compute_corr(h)
   F=C.max()
   best_h=h.copy(); best_F=F; best_C=C.copy()
   no_improve=0
   step=0
   # We may vary beta for gradient
   while step < max_steps and time.time() < deadline:
       F = float(C.max())
       if target_c5 is not None and F * (2.0/n) < target_c5: break
       mode = step % 10
       done=False
       if mode < 5:
           tol = max(0.25, 0.03*F)
           act = np.where(C >= F - tol)[0]
           if act.size ==0: act=np.array([int(np.argmax(C))])
           # choose weighted by deviation: active sorted? simple random among act
           idx = int(act[rng.randint(act.size)])
           p,q = sample_active_pair(h, idx, off, rng)
           if p is not None:
               curF=F
               t,newF = pair_line_search(h,C,p,q,0.0,1.0,grid=81)
               if t !=0 and newF < curF -1e-11:
                   done=True
       if not done and (mode >=5 or ...): # if active failed, try gradient
           if mode >=5 and mode <8:
           # Actually we can always fallback to gradient if active not done
           for beta in [8,20,4] maybe choose based no_improve
              g=accumulate_gradient(h,C,beta)
              p=int(np.argmin(g)); q=int(np.argmax(g))
              if p!=q:
                 curF=F
                 t,newF=pair_line_search(h,C,p,q,-np.inf,np.inf,grid=97)

def random_perturb_pairs(h, rng, n, num_pairs, amp=0.1):
    for _ in range(num_pairs):
       p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n)); while p==q: q=int(rng.randint(n))
       t=float(rng.uniform(-amp,amp))
       # bound t
       max_pos=min(1-h[p], h[q]); max_neg=-min(h[p],1-h[q])
       if t > max_pos: t=max_pos
       if t < max_neg: t=max_neg
       if abs(t)>1e-12:
          h[p]+=t; h[q]-=t
    return project_feasible(h,n)

def pair_line_search(h,C,p,q,t_min=-np.inf,t_max=np.inf,grid=97):
    n=len(h); off=n-1; m=2*n-1
    if p == q: return 0.0, float(np.max(C))
    currentF = float(np.max(C))
    max_pos = min(1.0 - h[p], h[q])
    max_neg = -min(h[p], 1.0 - h[q])
    lo = max(t_min, max_neg)
    hi = min(t_max, max_pos)
    # margin
    lo = max(lo, -1.0 + 1e-12)
    hi = min(hi, 1.0 - 1e-12)
    if hi - lo <= 1e-12:
        return 0.0, currentF
    # s
    s = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    add_point_derivative(s,h,p,1.0)
    add_point_derivative(s,h,q,-1.0)
    # Coarse grid. Include 0 if within interval by constructing union?
    # We can create tg = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid); if 0 in [lo,hi], ensure one element 0 by replacing closest.
    tg = np.linspace(lo, hi, int(grid))
    if 0.0 >= lo and 0.0 <= hi:
        idx0 = int(np.argmin(np.abs(tg))) # maybe not exact; set tg[idx0]=0.0
        tg[idx0]=0.0
    # Evaluate
    mat = C[None, :] + tg[:, None] * s[None, :]
    L = abs(p-q)
    if L > 0:
        mat[:, off + L] += tg * tg
        mat[:, off - L] += tg * tg
    ft = mat.max(axis=1)
    bi = int(np.argmin(ft))
    best_t = float(tg[bi]); best_val=float(ft[bi])
    if best_val >= currentF: # due grid, ensure t0
        # find value at 0 index if included
        if 0.0 >= lo and 0.0 <= hi:
            i0 = int(np.argmin(np.abs(tg)))
            best_val = float(ft[i0]); best_t=0.0
    if best_val < currentF - 1e-10:
        # refine around best_t if not too near endpoints
        if best_t != 0.0:
            span = (hi - lo) / float(len(tg))
            lo2 = max(lo, best_t - 2.0*span)
            hi2 = min(hi, best_t + 2.0*span)
            if hi2 - lo2 > 1e-12:
                tg2 = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 33)
                mat2 = C[None,:] + tg2[:,None]*s[None,:]
                if L>0:
                    mat2[:, off+L] += tg2*tg2; mat2[:, off-L] += tg2*tg2
                ft2=mat2.max(axis=1)
                bi2=int(np.argmin(ft2))
                if ft2[bi2] < best_val - 1e-13:
                    best_val=float(ft2[bi2]); best_t=float(tg2[bi2])
        # apply
        # ensure bounds
        t=best_t
        if t < max_neg - 1e-12 or t > max_pos +1e-12:
            # clip to valid
            t = float(np.clip(t, max_neg, max_pos))
        if abs(t) > 1e-13:
            C += t * s + (t*t) * (L? no we need add t^2 to two columns)
            if L>0:
                C[off+L] += t*t
                C[off-L] += t*t
            else:
                # no quadratic
                pass
            # But above C += t*s already includes linear at quadratic lags; correct.
            h[p] += t; h[q] -= t
            # Clip tiny
            if h[p] < 0.0: h[p]=0.0
            elif h[p] > 1.0: h[p]=1.0
            if h[q] < 0.0: h[q]=0.0
            elif h[q] > 1.0: h[q]=1.0
            C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
            # recompute actual max maybe
            newF = float(np.max(C))
            # If due grid error newF >= currentF, revert? We don't have previous h/C. To be safe, if newF >= currentF - 1e-9, revert manually? Need save old h values and C? We can save t and s? Easier: before apply, save h_old.copy? n small; C old copy? C length ~400. Could copy to revert if needed. But pair_line_search called often; copying every time overhead but acceptable? Maybe not. def accumulate_gradient(h,C,beta=8.0,max_lags=100):
   n=len(h); off=n-1; m=2*n-1
   F=C.max()
   # weights = exp(beta*(C-F)); to avoid underflow
   w = np.exp(np.clip(beta*(C-F), -50.0, 0.0))
   # If beta=0, w all ones; we'll cap max_lags.
   wsum=w.sum()
   if wsum <=0:
       idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(C))]); w=np.array([1.0])
   else:
       w=w/wsum
       # choose lags with largest weights
       order=np.argsort(w)[::-1]
       order=order[:max_lags]
       # renormalize over selected
       ws=w[order].sum(); wsel=w[order]/ws
   g=np.zeros(n)
   for kk,k in enumerate(order):
      wk=float(ysel? If using wsel align)
      L=abs(k-off)
      if L==0:
         g += wk*(1.0-2.0*h)
      elif k>off:
         r=n-L
         g[L:] += wk*(1.0-h[:r])
         g[:r] -= wk*h[L:]
      else:
         r=n-L
         g[:r] += wk*(1.0-h[L:])
         g[L:] -= wk*h[:r]
   return g
Need variables correctly. We'll make order, w_order.

Simpler previous is okay. We'll implement robust:
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float,copy=True); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(50):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            mask=h<1.0-1e-15
            cnt=int(mask.sum())
            if cnt==0: break
            add=diff/cnt
            # If add would push some beyond 1, use min positive room
            rooms=1.0-h[mask]
            max_room=rooms.min()
            if add > max_room:
                h[mask]+=max_room
            else:
                h[mask]+=add
        else:
            mask=h>1e-15
            cnt=int(mask.sum())
            if cnt==0: break
            add=diff/cnt # negative
            rooms=h[mask]
            min_room=rooms.min()
            if add < -min_room:
                h[mask]-=min_room
            else:
                h[mask]+=add
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
        if diff>0:
            idx=int(np.argmin(1.0-h))
        else:
            idx=int(np.argmin(h))
        h[idx]+=diff
        h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
This may leave sum off if min_room zero? But diff small. Good.

interp_h: if n_dst==n_src return project_feasible(h_src.copy(),n_dst). Else use centered coordinates.
def interp_h(h_src,n_dst):
   n_src=len(h_src)
   if n_src == 0: h=np.full(n_dst,0.5); return project_feasible(h,n_dst)
   if n_src == n_dst: return project_feasible(h_src.copy(), n_dst)
   x_src=(np.arange(n_src)+0.5)*(2.0/n_src)
   x_dst=(np.arange(n_dst)+0.5)*(2.0/n_dst)
   h_dst=np.interp(x_dst, x_src, h_src)
   return project_feasible(h_dst, n_dst)

binary_active_search code specifics:
def binary_active_search(h, target_count, max_trials, deadline, rng):
    n=len(h); off=n-1; m=2*n-1
    h=threshold_to_binary(h,n).copy()
    C=compute_corr(h)
    bestF=float(np.max(C)); best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy(); best_sumsq=float(np.dot(C,C))
    curF=bestF
    no_improve=0; trial=0; K=min(8,m); top_cache=np.arange(m)
    while trial < max_trials and time.time() < deadline:
        # refresh top every 32 or if no cache
        if trial % 32 == 0:
            top_cache = np.argpartition(C, -K)[-K:] if m>K else np.arange(m)
            curF=float(np.max(C))
        if no_improve > 3000:
            # reset to best and random perturb
            h=best_h.copy(); C=best_C.copy(); curF=bestF
            # random flips count 3+rand
            for _ in range(int(rng.randint(3, 10))):
                 p1=None; r=... rejection
                 p0 similarly
                 if p1 is not None and p0 is not None and p1!=p0:
                      flip_binary(h,C,p1,-1.0); flip_binary(h,C,p0,+1.0)
            C=compute_corr(h)
            curF=float(np.max(C)); no_improve=0; top_cache=...
        idx=int(top_cache[rng.randint(len(top_cache))])
        L=abs(idx-off)
        if L == 0:
           idx=int(np.argmax(C)); L=abs(idx-off)
        p_inc=-1; p_dec=-1
        r=n-L
        if r <=0: continue? L<n, r>=1.
        if idx > off:
           found=False
           for _ in range(8):
               i=int(rng.randint(r))
               if h[i] == 0.0 and h[i+L] == 1.0:
                   p_inc=i; p_dec=i+L; found=True; break
           if not found:
               vals=h[L:n]*(1.0-h[:r])
               i=int(np.argmax(vals))
               if vals[i] > 0.5:
                   p_inc=i; p_dec=i+L
               else:
                   # choose random pair maybe
                   p_inc=-1
        else:
           for _ in range(8):
               i=int(rng.randint(r))
               if h[i] == 1.0 and h[i+L] == 0.0:
                   p_inc=i+L; p_dec=i; found=True; break
           if not found: vals=h[:r]*(1.0-h[L:n]); i=argmax; if vals[i]>0.5: p_inc=i+L;p_dec=i
        if p_inc <0 or p_dec<0:
            # random pair flip
            p_inc = random zero; p_dec=random one; if none no_improve
        if p_inc <0 or p_dec<0 or p_inc==p_dec:
            no_improve +=1; trial+=1; continue
        # apply
        flip_binary(h,C,p_inc,1.0); flip_binary(h,C,p_dec,-1.0)
        newF=float(np.max(C))
        improved=False; accepted=False
        if newF + 1e-8 < bestF:
            accepted=True; improved=True
        elif abs(newF - bestF) <= 1e-7:
            sumsq=float(np.dot(C,C))
            if sumsq < best_sumsq - 1e-7:
                accepted=True; improved=True
            elif rng.random() < 0.05:
                accepted=True
        if accepted:
            C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
            if improved:
                bestF=newF; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy(); best_sumsq=float(np.dot(C,C)); no_improve=0
                if int(round(bestF)) <= target_count + 1e-9 and bestF <= target_count + 1e-9:
                    break
            # else wander
        else:
            flip_binary(h,C,p_inc,-1.0); flip_binary(h,C,p_dec,+1.0)
            C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
            no_improve +=1
        trial +=1
    C=compute_corr(best_h)
    return best_h, float(np.max(C))

def random_zero_one(h, rng, n):
   z=-1
   for _ in range(20): z=int(rng.randint(n)); if h[z]==0: break
   o=-1
   for _ in range(20): o=int(rng.randint(n)); if h[o]==1 and o!=z: break
   return z,o
But if no, okay. Use in binary.

Function:
def continuous_pair_opt(h, max_steps, deadline, rng, target_c5=None):
    n=len(h); h=project_feasible(h.copy(), n)
    C=compute_corr(h); F=float(C.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_F=F; best_C=C.copy()
    target_F = None if target_c5 is None else target_c5 * n / 2.0
    off=n-1
    no_improve=0; step=0
    while step < max_steps and time.time() < deadline:
        if target_F is not None and best_F <= target_F + 1e-12:
            break
        F=float(C.max())
        done=False
        mode = step % 3
        if mode == 0:
            tol=max(0.25,0.03*F)
            act=np.where(C >= F - tol)[0]
            if act.size==0: act=np.array([int(np.argmax(C))])
            idx=int(act[rng.randint(act.size)])
            p,q=sample_active_pair(h,idx,off,rng)
            if p is not None:
                t,newF=pair_line_search(h,C,p,q,0.0,1.0,grid=89)
                if t != 0.0 and newF < F -1e-10:
                    F=newF; done=True
        else:
            beta = 6.0 + (step % 5)*3.0
            g=accumulate_gradient(h,C,beta,80)
            p=int(np.argmin(g)); q=int(np.argmax(g))
            if p != q:
                t,newF=pair_line_search(h,C,p,q,-np.inf,np.inf,grid=121)
                if t != 0.0 and newF < F -1e-10:
                    F=newF; done=True
        if not done:
            # random low/high pair attempt a few
            best_localF=F
            for _ in range(3):
                p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
                while p==q: q=int(rng.randint(n))
                # prefer p lower h, q higher h
                if h[p] > h[q]: p,q=q,p
                t,newF=pair_line_search(h,C,p,q,0.0,1.0,grid=73) # only p inc q dec; try both? If after swap p lower.
                if t !=0 and newF < best_localF -1e-10:
                    best_localF=newF; done=True; break
            # Also could full interval random:
            if not done:
                p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n)); while p==q: q=int(rng.randint(n))
                t,newF=pair_line_search(h,C,p,q,-np.inf,np.inf,grid=65)
                if t !=0 and newF < F -1e-10:
                    F=newF; done=True
        if done:
            F=float(C.max())
            if F < best_F -1e-11:
                best_F=F; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy(); no_improve=0
            else:
                no_improve +=1
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve > 500:
                # perturb from best
                h=random_perturb_pairs(best_h, rng, n, int(rng.randint(5,30)), 0.08)
                C=compute_corr(h); F=float(C.max())
                no_improve=0
                # if too bad, reset
                if F > best_F + max(3.0, 0.05*best_F):
                    # try smaller amp? just keep; might improve
                    pass
        if step % 100 == 0:
            # correct drift
            C2=compute_corr(h)
            F2=float(C2.max())
            if F2 < C.max() + 1e-6:
                C=C2; F=F2
            if F > best_F + max(5.0,0.1*best_F):
                h=best_h.copy(); C=best_C.copy(); F=best_F
        step +=1
    return best_h,best_F

Need random_perturb_pairs top-level:
def random_perturb_pairs(h, rng, n, num_pairs, amp):
    h=h.copy()
    for _ in range(num_pairs):
       p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n)); while p==q: q=int(rng.randint(n))
       t=float(rng.uniform(-amp,amp))
       max_pos=min(1.0-h[p], h[q]); max_neg=-min(h[p],1.0-h[q])
       if t > max_pos: t=max_pos
       if t < max_neg: t=max_neg
       if abs(t)>1e-12:
          h[p]+=t; h[q]-=t
    return project_feasible(h,n)

If first attempt no improvement, C unchanged; second okay. If first improves, we break and set done; F updated. Good.

But pair_line_search with t_min=0 only allows p inc. We swap p lower h, q higher h, so moving mass from high to low (increase low). That's generally good for C0 but may not. Full interval random tries both.

Now, accumulate_gradient: It processes max_lags selected by weights. Need if beta large and active set small. Use max_lags=120.
Code:
def accumulate_gradient(h,C,beta=8.0,max_lags=90):
    n=len(h); off=n-1
    F=C.max()
    w=np.exp(np.clip(beta*(C-F), -50.0, 0.0))
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws <= 1e-300:
       sel=np.array([int(np.argmax(C))]); wsel=np.array([1.0])
    else:
       order=np.argsort(w)[::-1]
       sel=order[:min(max_lags,len(order))]
       wsel=w[sel]/w[sel].sum()
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for kk, k in enumerate(sel):
       wk=float(wsel[kk]); L=abs(k-off); r=n-L
       if L==0:
          g += wk*(1.0-2.0*h)
       elif k>off:
          g[L:] += wk*(1.0-h[:r])
          g[:r] -= wk*h[L:]
       else:
          g[:r] += wk*(1.0-h[L:])
          g[L:] -= wk*h[:r]
    return g
For max_lags maybe 120; if C length small. Good.

sample_active_pair returns tuple; if fails None None.

Pseudo:
def optimize_n(n, parent_h, rng, deadline, target_c5, best_h, best_c5, best_n):
    dx=2.0/n
    targetF=target_c5*n/2.0
    target_count=int(np.floor(target_c5 * (n/2.0) + 1e-12))
    # attempt starts
    starts=[]
    if parent_h is not None:
       starts.append(interp_h(parent_h,n))
    if best_h is not None and len(best_h)>0:
       starts.append(interp_h(best_h,n))
    starts.append(make_smooth_random(n,rng))
    starts.append(make_block_binary(n,rng))
    starts.append(make_random_binary(n,rng))
    # Deduplicate? not necessary, but if many, limit attempts
    attempts = min(5, len(starts))
    # Give each attempt a sub_deadline based on remaining
    for a in range(attempts):
       if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
       remaining_n = deadline - time.time()
       # If many attempts left, allocate e.g. max(20, 0.7*remaining_n/attempts_left)
       attempts_left = attempts - a
       alloc = min(120.0, max(25.0, 0.8*remaining_n / attempts_left))
       sub_deadline = time.time() + alloc
       # continuous from start
       h0=project_feasible(starts[a].copy(), n)
       cont_deadline = min(sub_deadline, time.time()+0.45*alloc)
       h,F=continuous_pair_opt(h0, 10000, cont_deadline, rng, target_c5)
       c5=compute_c5(h)

def random_zero_one(h,rng,n):
   z=-1; o=-1
   for _ in range(50):
      i=int(rng.randint(n))
      if h[i] < 0.5 and z<0: z=i
      if h[i] > 0.5 and o<0 and i!=z: o=i
      if z>=0 and o>=0: break
   # If not found, fallback flatnonzero
   if z<0:
      zs=np.flatnonzero(h<0.5); z=int(zs[rng.randint(len(zs))]) if len(zs) else -1
   if o<0 or o==z:
      os=np.flatnonzero(h>0.5)
      # choose not z
      if len(os)==0: o=-1
      else:
         o=int(os[rng.randint(len(os))])
         if o==z: o=int(os[(rng.randint(len(os))+1)%len(os)])
   return z,o

```python
import time
import numpy as np


def compute_corr(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").astype(float, copy=False)


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    c = compute_corr(h)
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n

    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break

        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-15
            cnt = int(np.sum(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            add = diff / cnt
            max_room = float(np.min(1.0 - h[mask]))
            if add > max_room:
                h[mask] += max_room
            else:
                h[mask] += add
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-15
            cnt = int(np.sum(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            add = diff / cnt
            min_room = float(np.min(h[mask]))
            if add < -min_room:
                h[mask] -= min_room
            else:
                h[mask] += add

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmin(1.0 - h))
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
        h[idx] += diff
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def interp_h(h_src, n_dst):
    h_src = np.asarray(h_src, dtype=float)
    n_src = len(h_src)
    if n_dst <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n_src == 0:
        h = np.full(n_dst, 0.5, dtype=float)
        return project_feasible(h, n_dst)
    if n_src == n_dst:
        return project_feasible(h_src.copy(), n_dst)

    x_src = (np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_src)
    x_dst = (np.arange(n_dst) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_dst)
    h_dst = np.interp(x_dst, x_src, h_src)
    return project_feasible(h_dst, n_dst)


def threshold_to_binary(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2

    closed = np.rint(h)
    if np.all(np.abs(h - closed) < 1e-10) and int(np.sum(closed)) == target:
        return closed.astype(float)

    if target <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    part = np.argpartition(-h, target - 1)[:target]
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    out[part] = 1.0
    return out


def make_random_binary(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2
    if target > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=target, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def make_smooth_random(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    for _ in range(3):
        freq = rng.uniform(0.5, 4.0)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        amp = rng.uniform(0.12, 0.32)
        h += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_block_binary(n, rng):
    R = int(rng.randint(8, min(40, max(8, n // 2)) + 1))
    start = int(rng.randint(2))

    if R - 1 <= 0:
        cuts = np.array([], dtype=int)
    else:
        cuts = np.sort(rng.choice(np.arange(1, n), size=min(R - 1, n - 1), replace=False))

    lengths = np.diff(np.concatenate(([0], cuts, [n])))
    if len(lengths) < R:
        rem = R - len(lengths)
        pos = list(rng.choice(np.arange(1, n), size=min(rem, n - 1), replace=False))
        lengths = np.insert(lengths, pos, 1)
        lengths = lengths[:R]

    bits = np.where((np.arange(len(lengths)) % 2) == start, 1.0, 0.0)
    h = np.repeat(bits, lengths)
    h = h[:n]

    target = n // 2
    cur = int(np.sum(h())) if callable(h) else int(np.sum(h))
    diff = target - cur
    if diff > 0:
        zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
        if len(zeros) >= diff:
            flip = rng.choice(zeros, size=diff, replace=False)
            h[flip] = 1.0
    elif diff < 0:
        ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
        if len(ones) >= -diff:
            flip = rng.choice(ones, size=-diff, replace=False)
            h[flip] = 0.0

    return project_feasible(h, n)


def sym_variants(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    comp = 1.0 - h
    return [
        h.copy(),
        h[::-1].copy(),
        comp.copy(),
        comp[::-1].copy(),
    ]


def add_point_derivative(s, h, x, sign):
    n = len(h)
    off = n - 1

    s[off] += sign * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[x])

    if x > 0:
        s[off + 1:off + x + 1] += sign * (1.0 - h[:x])[::-1]

    r = n - 1 - x
    if r > 0:
        s[off + 1:off + r + 1] += -sign * h[x + 1:n]
        s[x:off] += sign * (1.0 - h[x + 1:n])[::-1]
        s[off - x:off] += -sign * h[:x]


def pair_line_search(h, C, p, q, t_min=-np.inf, t_max=np.inf, grid=97):
    n = len(h)
    off = n - 1
    m = 2 * n - 1

    currentF = float(np.max(C))
    if p == q:
        return 0.0, currentF

    max_pos = min(1.0 - h[p], h[q])
    max_neg = -min(h[p], 1.0 - h[q])

    lo = max(t_min, max_neg)
    hi = min(t_max, max_pos)

    lo = max(lo, -1.0 + 1e-12)
    hi = min(hi, 1.0 - 1e-12)

    if hi - lo <= 1e-12:
        return 0.0, currentF

    s = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    add_point_derivative(s, h, p, 1.0)
    add_point_derivative(s, h, q, -1.0)

    tg = np.linspace(lo, hi, int(max(33, min(193, grid))))
    if 0.0 >= lo and 0.0 <= hi:
        i0 = int(np.argmin(np.abs(tg)))
        tg[i0] = 0.0

    mat = C[None, :] + tg[:, None] * s[None, :]
    t2 = tg * tg
    L = abs(p - q)
    if L > 0:
        mat[:, off + L] += t2
        mat[:, off - L] += t2
    mat[:, off] -= 2.0 * t2

    ft = mat.max(axis=1)
    bi = int(np.argmin(ft))
    best_t = float(tg[bi])
    best_val = float(ft[bi])

    if 0.0 >= lo and 0.0 <= hi:
        i0 = int(np.argmin(np.abs(tg)))
        if float(ft[i0]) < best_val + 1e-15:
            best_t = 0.0
            best_val = float(ft[i0])

    if best_val < currentF - 1e-10:
        if best_t != 0.0:
            span = (hi - lo) / float(len(tg))
            lo2 = max(lo, best_t - 2.0 * span)
            hi2 = min(hi, best_t + 2.0 * span)
            if hi2 - lo2 > 1e-12:
                tg2 = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 33)
                mat2 = C[None, :] + tg2[:, None] * s[None, :]
                tt2 = tg2 * tg2
                if L > 0:
                    mat2[:, off + L] += tt2
                    mat2[:, off - L] += tt2
                mat2[:, off] -= 2.0 * tt2
                ft2 = mat2.max(axis=1)
                bi2 = int(np.argmin(ft2))
                if float(ft2[bi2]) < best_val - 1e-14:
                    best_val = float(ft2[bi2])
                    best_t = float(tg2[bi2])

        t = float(best_t)
        t = float(np.clip(t, max_neg, max_pos))
        if abs(t) > 1e-13:
            oldC = C.copy()
            oldp = h[p]
            oldq = h[q]

            C += t * s
            tt = t * t
            if L > 0:
                C[off + L] += tt
                C[off - L] += tt
            C[off] -= 2.0 * tt

            h[p] = oldp + t
            h[q] = oldq - t

            if h[p] < 0.0:
                h[p] = 0.0
            elif h[p] > 1.0:
                h[p] = 1.0
            if h[q] < 0.0:
                h[q] = 0.0
            elif h[q] > 1.0:
                h[q] = 1.0

            C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
            newF = float(np.max(C))

            if newF >= currentF - 1e-9:
                C[:] = oldC
                h[p] = oldp
                h[q] = oldq
                return 0.0, currentF

            return t, newF

    return 0.0, currentF


def accumulate_gradient(h, C, beta=8.0, max_lags=90):
    n = len(h)
    off = n - 1
    F = float(np.max(C))

    w = np.exp(np.clip(beta * (C - F), -60.0, 0.0))
    ws = float(np.sum(w))

    if ws <= 1e-300:
        sel = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))], dtype=int)
        wsel = np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
    else:
        order = np.argsort(w)[::-1]
        sel = order[:max(1, min(max_lags, len(order)))]
        wsel = w[sel]
        sm = float(np.sum(wsel))
        if sm > 0.0:
            wsel = wsel / sm
        else:
            wsel = np.ones(len(sel), dtype=float) / len(sel)

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for kk, k in enumerate(sel):
        wk = float(wsel[kk])
        L = abs(k - off)
        r = n - L
        if L == 0:
            g += wk * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif k > off:
            g[L:] += wk * (1.0 - h[:r])
            g[:r] -= wk * h[L:]
        else:
            g[:r] += wk * (1.0 - h[L:])
            g[L:] -= wk * h[:r]
    return g


def sample_active_pair(h, idx, off, rng):
    n = len(h)
    L = abs(idx - off)
    if L == 0:
        return None, None

    r = n - L
    if r <= 0:
        return None, None

    if idx > off:
        vals = h[L:n] * (1.0 - h[:r])
        for _ in range(6):
            i = int(rng.randint(r))
            if vals[i] > 1e-9:
                return i, i + L
        i = int(np.argmax(vals))
        if vals[i] < 1e-11:
            return None, None
        return i, i + L

    vals = h[:r] * (1.0 - h[L:n])
    for _ in range(6):
        i = int(rng.randint(r))
        if vals[i] > 1e-9:
            return i + L, i
    i = int(np.argmax(vals))
    if vals[i] < 1e-11:
        return None, None
    return i + L, i


def random_perturb_pairs(h, rng, n, num_pairs, amp):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    for _ in range(int(num_pairs)):
        p = int(rng.randint(n))
        q = int(rng.randint(n))
        while p == q:
            q = int(rng.randint(n))

        t = float(rng.uniform(-amp, amp))
        max_pos = min(1.0 - h[p], h[q])
        max_neg = -min(h[p], 1.0 - h[q])
        if t > max_pos:
            t = max_pos
        if t < max_neg:
            t = max_neg

        if abs(t) > 1e-12:
            h[p] += t
            h[q] -= t

    return project_feasible(h, n)


def continuous_pair_opt(h, max_steps, deadline, rng, target_c5=None):
    n = len(h)
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    C = compute_corr(h)
    F = float(np.max(C))

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_F = F
    best_C = C.copy()
    off = n - 1

    target_F = None
    if target_c5 is not None:
        target_F = target_c5 * (0.5 * n)

    no_improve = 0
    step = 0

    while step < max_steps and time.time() < deadline:
        if target_F is not None and best_F <= target_F + 1e-12:
            break

        F = float(np.max(C))
        done = False
        mode = step % 3

        if mode == 0:
            tol = max(0.25, 0.03 * F)
            act = np.where(C >= F - tol)[0]
            if act.size == 0:
                act = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))], dtype=int)
            idx = int(act[int(rng.randint(act.size))])

            p, q = sample_active_pair(h, idx, off, rng)
            if p is not None:
                t, newF = pair_line_search(h, C, p, q, 0.0, 1.0, grid=89)
                if t != 0.0 and newF < F - 1e-10:
                    F = newF
                    done = True

        elif mode == 1:
            beta = 4.0 + float(step % 6) * 6.0
            g = accumulate_gradient(h, C, beta=beta, max_lags=110)
            p = int(np.argmin(g))
            q = int(np.argmax(g))
            if p != q:
                t, newF = pair_line_search(h, C, p, q, -np.inf, np.inf, grid=121)
                if t != 0.0 and newF < F - 1e-10:
                    F = newF
                    done = True

        if not done:
            localF = F
            for _ in range(3):
                p = int(rng.randint(n))
                q = int(rng.randint(n))
                while p == q:
                    q = int(rng.randint(n))
                if h[p] > h[q]:
                    p, q = q, p
                t, newF = pair_line_search(h, C, p, q, 0.0, 1.0, grid=73)
                if t != 0.0 and newF < localF - 1e-10:
                    localF = newF
                    F = newF
                    done = True
                    break

            if not done:
                p = int(rng.randint(n))
                q = int(rng.randint(n))
                while p == q:
                    q = int(rng.randint(n))
                t, newF = pair_line_search(h, C, p, q, -np.inf, np.inf, grid=65)
                if t != 0.0 and newF < F - 1e-10:
                    F = newF
                    done = True

        if done:
            F = float(np.max(C))
            if F < best_F - 1e-11:
                best_F = F
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_C = C.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 350:
                h = random_perturb_pairs(best_h, rng, n, int(rng.randint(5, 35)), 0.07)
                C = compute_corr(h)
                F = float(np.max(C))
                no_improve = 0
                if F > best_F + max(4.0, 0.06 * best_F):
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    C = best_C.copy()
                    F = best_F

        if step % 120 == 0:
            C2 = compute_corr(h)
            F2 = float(np.max(C2))
            if F2 <= float(np.max(C)) + 1e-6:
                C = C2
                F = F2
            if F > best_F + max(6.0, 0.10 * best_F):
                h = best_h.copy()
                C = best_C.copy()
                F = best_F

        step += 1

    return best_h, best_F


def flip_binary(h, C, p, delta):
    n = len(h)
    off = n - 1
    d = float(delta)

    if d == 0.0:
        return

    if p > 0:
        C[off + 1:off + p + 1] += d * (1.0 - h[:p])[::-1]

    r = n - 1 - p
    if r > 0:
        C[off + 1:off + r + 1] += -d * h[p + 1:n]
        C[p:off] += d * (1.0 - h[p + 1:n])[::-1]
        C[off - p:off] += -d * h[:p]

    h[p] += d
    if h[p] < 0.5:
        h[p] = 0.0
    else:
        h[p] = 1.0


def random_zero_one(h, rng, n):
    z = -1
    o = -1

    for _ in range(80):
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        if h[i] < 0.5 and z < 0:
            z = i
        if h[i] > 0.5 and o < 0 and i != z:
            o = i
        if z >= 0 and o >= 0:
            break

    if z < 0:
        zs = np.flatnonzero(h < 0.5)
        if len(zs) == 0:
            return -1, -1
        z = int(zs[int(rng.randint(len(zs)))])

    if o < 0 or o == z:
        os = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
        if len(os) == 0:
            return z, -1
        o = int(os[int(rng.randint(len(os)))])
        if o == z:
            o = int(os[(int(rng.randint(len(os))) + 1) % len(os)])

    return z, o


def binary_active_search(h, target_count, max_trials, deadline, rng):
    n = len(h)
    off = n - 1
    m = 2 * n - 1

    h = threshold_to_binary(h, n).copy()
    C = compute_corr(h)

    bestF = float(np.max(C))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_C = C.copy()
    best_sumsq = float(np.dot(C, C))

    K = min(10, m)
    if K >= m:
        top_cache = np.arange(m, dtype=int)
    else:
        top_cache = np.argpartition(C, -K)[-K:]

    trial = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while trial < max_trials and time.time() < deadline:
        if trial % 24 == 0:
            if K >= m:
                top_cache = np.arange(m, dtype=int)
            else:
                top_cache = np.argpartition(C, -K)[-K:]

        if no_improve > 2600:
            h = best_h.copy()
            C = best_C.copy()
            for _ in range(int(rng.randint(4, 16))):
                z, o = random_zero_one(h, rng, n)
                if z >= 0 and o >= 0:
                    flip_binary(h, C, z, 1.0)
                    flip_binary(h, C, o, -1.0)
            C = compute_corr(h)
            C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
            no_improve = 0
            if K >= m:
                top_cache = np.arange(m, dtype=int)
            else:
                top_cache = np.argpartition(C, -K)[-K:]

        idx = int(top_cache[int(rng.randint(len(top_cache)))])
        L = abs(idx - off)
        if L == 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(C))
            L = abs(idx - off)
        if L == 0 or L >= n:
            no_improve += 1
            trial += 1
            continue

        r = n - L
        p_inc = -1
        p_dec = -1

        found = False
        if idx > off:
            for _ in range(10):
                i = int(rng.randint(r))
                if h[i] < 0.5 and h[i + L] > 0.5:
                    p_inc = i
                    p_dec = i + L
                    found = True
                    break
            if not found:
                vals = h[L:n] * (1.0 - h[:r])
                i = int(np.argmax(vals))
                if vals[i] > 0.5:
                    p_inc = i
                    p_dec = i + L
        else:
            for _ in range(10):
                i = int(rng.randint(r))
                if h[i] > 0.5 and h[i + L] < 0.5:
                    p_inc = i + L
                    p_dec = i
                    found = True
                    break
            if not found:
                vals = h[:r] * (1.0 - h[L:n])
                i = int(np.argmax(vals))
                if vals[i] > 0.5:
                    p_inc = i + L
                    p_dec = i

        if p_inc < 0 or p_dec < 0:
            z, o = random_zero_one(h, rng, n)
            if z >= 0 and o >= 0:
                p_inc = z
                p_dec = o

        if p_inc < 0 or p_dec < 0 or p_inc == p_dec:
            no_improve += 1
            trial += 1
            continue

        flip_binary(h, C, p_inc, 1.0)
        flip_binary(h, C, p_dec, -1.0)
        C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)

        newF = float(np.max(C))

        accepted = False
        improved = False

        if newF + 1e-8 < bestF:
            accepted = True
            improved = True
        elif abs(newF - bestF) <= 1e-7:
            sumsq = float(np.dot(C, C))
            if sumsq < best_sumsq - 1e-7:
                accepted = True
                improved = True
            elif rng.random() < 0.04:
                accepted = True

        if accepted:
            if improved:
                bestF = newF
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_C = C.copy()
                best_sumsq = float(np.dot(C, C))
                no_improve = 0
                if int(round(bestF)) <= target_count + 1e-9 and bestF <= target_count + 1e-9:
                    break
        else:
            flip_binary(h, C, p_inc, -1.0)
            flip_binary(h, C, p_dec, 1.0)
            C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
            no_improve += 1

        trial += 1

    Cb = compute_corr(best_h)
    return best_h, float(np.max(Cb))


def optimize_n(n, parent_h, rng, deadline, target_c5, best_h, best_c5, best_n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    target_count = int(np.floor(target_c5 * (0.5 * n) + 1e-12))

    starts = []
    if parent_h is not None and len(parent_h) > 0:
        starts.append(interp_h(parent_h, n))
    if best_h is not None and len(best_h) > 0:
        starts.append(interp_h(best_h, n))
    starts.append(make_smooth_random(n, rng))
    starts.append(make_block_binary(n, rng))
    starts.append(make_random_binary(n, rng))

    starts = raises = starts[:5]  # harmless alias, keeps list local
    attempts = len(starts)

    for a in range(attempts):
        if time.time() > deadline - 20:
            break

        remaining_n = max(1.0, deadline - time.time())
        attempts_left = max(1, attempts - a)
        alloc = min(130.0, max(22.0, 0.85 * remaining_n / float(attempts_left)))
        sub_deadline = min(deadline - 5.0, time.time() + alloc)
        if sub_deadline <= time.time() + 10.0:
            break

        h0 = project_feasible(np.array(starts[a], dtype=float, copy=True), n)

        cont_deadline = min(sub_deadline, time.time() + max(8.0, 0.50 * alloc))
        h_cont, _ = continuous_pair_opt(h0, 100000, cont_deadline, rng, target_c5)
        c5c = compute_c5(h_cont)
        if c5c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = c5c
            best_h = h_cont.copy()
            best_n = n

        h_bin = threshold_to_binary(h_cont, n)

        bin_deadline = min(sub_deadline, time.time() + max(5.0, 0.32 * alloc))
        h_bin, _ = binary_active_search(h_bin, target_count, 1000000, bin_deadline, rng)
        c5b = compute_c5(h_bin)
        if c5b < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = c5b
            best_h = h_bin.copy()
            best_n = n

        h_bin2 = threshold_to_binary(np.array(starts[a], dtype=float), n)
        if not np.allclose(h_bin2, h_bin, atol=1e-12, rtol=0.0):
            deadline2 = min(sub_deadline, time.time() + max(4.0, 0.12 * alloc))
            if deadline2 > time.time() + 6.0:
                h_bin2, _ = binary_active_search(h_bin2, target_count, 200000, deadline2, rng)
                c5b2 = compute_c5(h_bin2)
                if c5b2 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_c5 = c5b2
                    best_h = h_bin2.copy()
                    best_n = n
                    h_bin = h_bin2

        cont_deadline2 = min(sub_deadline, time.time() + max(4.0, 0.12 * alloc))
        if cont_deadline2 > time.time() + 6.0:
            h_rep, _ = continuous_pair_opt(h_bin, 30000, cont_deadline2, rng, target_c5)
            c5r = compute_c5(h_rep)
            if c5r < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5 = c5r
                best_h = h_rep.copy()
                best_n = n

    return best_h, best_c5, best_n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = start_time + max(5.0, budget_s - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    target_c5 = 0.38085

    parent_h = None
    try:
        parent_h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float, copy=True)
        parent_n = len(parent_h)
        parent_h = project_feasible(parent_h, parent_n)
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None
        parent_n = 0

    if parent_h is not None:
        best_h = parent_h.copy()
        best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)
        best_n = parent_n
    else:
        best_n = 200
        best_h = make_smooth_random(best_n, rng)
        best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    for var in sym_variants(best_h):
        c5v = compute_c5(var)
        if c5v < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = c5v
            best_h = var
            best_n = len(var)

    n_order_base = [
        parent_n if parent_n is not None and 64 <= parent_n <= 600 else 200,
        160, 200, 240, 256, 128, 320, 384, 448,
    ]

    n_order = []
    for nn in n_order_base:
        nn = int(nn)
        if nn % 2 == 0 and 64 <= nn <= 512 and nn not in n_order:
            n_order.append(nn)

    for rank, n in enumerate(n_order):
        if time.time() > deadline - 35:
            break

        if rank < 2:
            alloc = 270.0
        elif rank < 4:
            alloc = 200.0
        else:
            alloc = 110.0

        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
        if remaining < 45.0:
            break

        alloc = min(alloc, remaining - 30.0)
        if alloc < 45.0:
            alloc = remaining - 25.0
            if alloc < 35.0:
                break

        deadline_n = time.time() + alloc
        best_h, best_c5, best_n = optimize_n(
            n,
            parent_h,
            rng,
            deadline_n,
            target_c5,
            best_h,
            best_c5,
            best_n,
        )

    if time.time() < deadline - 30:
        n = int(best_n)

        cont_deadline = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 55.0)
        h0 = interp_h(best_h, n)
        hh, _ = continuous_pair_opt(h0, 100000, cont_deadline, rng, target_c5)
        c5new = compute_c5(hh)
        if c5new < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = c5new
            best_h = hh
            best_n = n

        if time.time() < deadline - 25:
            hbin = threshold_to_binary(best_h, n)
            target_count = int(np.floor(target_c5 * (0.5 * n) + 1e-12))
            bin_deadline = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 35.0)
            hbin, _ = binary_active_search(hbin, target_count, 500000, bin_deadline, rng)
            c5bin = compute_c5(hbin)
            if c5bin < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5 = c5bin
                best_h = hbin
                best_n = n

        if time.time() < deadline - 12:
            hh2, _ = continuous_pair_opt(hbin, 30000, min(deadline - 5.0, time.time() + 20.0), rng, target_c5)
            c5bin2 = compute_c5(hh2)
            if c5bin2 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5 = c5bin2
                best_h = hh2
                best_n = n

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, int(best_n))
    final_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    h_bin_final = threshold_to_binary(best_h, int(best_n))
    c5_bin_final = compute_c5(h_bin_final)
    if c5_bin_final < final_c5 - 1e-12:
        final_c5 = c5_bin_final
        best_h = h_bin_final

    for var in sym_variants(best_h):
        c5v = compute_c5(var)
        if c5v < final_c5 - 1e-12:
            final_c5 = c5v
            best_h = var

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, int(best_n))
    final_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    try:
        ev = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if ev is not None and np.isfinite(ev) and float(ev) < final_c5 - 1e-15:
            final_c5 = float(ev)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, float(final_c5), int(best_n))
```
